6. **No lipid-binding motifs**: No PH domains, C1/C2 domains, or other lipid-binding motifs.

7. **The overall composition** - high in proline, glycine, glutamine, and alanine with tandem CCCH zinc fingers - is very characteristic of RNA-binding proteins, particularly those in the tristetraprolin family (like ZFP36/TTP, ZFP36L1, ZFP36L2) or similar CCCH zinc finger-containing RNA-binding proteins.

Given all these observations, the best answer is A. RNA binding.
